  "content": "1: # Changelog\n2: \n3: All notable changes to rust-editor are documented in this file.\n4: \n5: ---\n6: \n7: ## 2025-12-06 (Latest)\n8: \n9: ### Added - Split View Implementation (All 7 Phases)\n10: \n11: Complete multi-pane editor with split views, tabs, and shared documents.\n12: \n13: #### Phase 1: Core Data Structures\n14: - `DocumentId`, `EditorId`, `GroupId`, `TabId` - typed identifiers\n15: - `Tab` struct with editor reference, pinned/preview flags\n16: - `EditorGroup` with tabs, active tab index, layout rect\n17: - `LayoutNode` enum: `Group(GroupId)` or `Split(SplitContainer)`\n18: - `SplitContainer` with direction, children, ratios, min_sizes\n19: - `EditorArea` managing documents, editors, groups, and layout tree\n20: \n21: #### Phase 2: Layout System\n22: - `Rect` type for layout calculations with `contains()` hit testing\n23: - `compute_layout()` recursive algorithm for layout tree\n24: - `group_at_point()` for mouse hit testing\n25: - `SplitterBar` struct for splitter positions\n26: - `splitter_at_point()` for resize handle detection\n27: - `SPLITTER_WIDTH` constant (4px)\n28: \n29: #### Phase 3: AppModel Migration\n30: - Replaced single `Document`/`EditorState` with `EditorArea`\n31: - Backward-compatible accessor methods: `document()`, `editor()`, etc.\n32: - `ensure_focused_cursor_visible()` helper avoiding document cloning\n33: - `resize()` now updates ALL editors (fixes multi-pane viewport bug)\n34: \n35: #### Phase 4: LayoutMsg Handlers\n36: - `SplitFocused(SplitDirection)` - split current group\n37: - `SplitGroup { group_id, direction }` - split specific group\n38: - `CloseGroup`, `CloseFocusedGroup` - close with layout cleanup\n39: - `FocusGroup`, `FocusNextGroup`, `FocusPrevGroup` - navigation\n40: - `FocusGroupByIndex(usize)` - keyboard shortcuts (1-indexed)\n41: - `CloseTab`, `CloseFocusedTab`, `MoveTab` - tab operations\n42: - `NextTab`, `PrevTab`, `SwitchToTab` - tab navigation\n43: \n44: #### Phase 5: Multi-Group Rendering\n45: - `render_all_groups_static()` iterates over layout\n46: - `render_editor_group_static()` renders single pane\n47: - Tab bar rendering with active/inactive styling\n48: - Splitter bar rendering between groups\n49: - Focus indicator (border) on focused group\n50: \n51: #### Phase 6: Document Synchronization\n52: - Documents shared across views (same `DocumentId`)\n53: - Independent cursor/viewport per `EditorState`\n54: - Edits reflect immediately in all views of same document\n55: \n56: #### Phase 7: Keyboard Shortcuts\n57: - Numpad 1-4: Focus group by index\n58: - Numpad -/+: Split horizontal/vertical\n59: - Cmd+W: Close tab\n60: - Option+Cmd+Left/Right: Previous/Next tab\n61: - Ctrl+Tab: Focus next group\n62: - `physical_key` support in `handle_key()` for numpad detection\n63: \n64: ### Fixed - Split View Bugs\n65: \n66: - `close_tab` on last group's only tab now prevented (was leaving invalid state)\n67: - `move_tab` to invalid group now no-op (was losing tabs)\n68: - Viewport resize updates all editors, not just focused one\n69: \n70: ### Added - Performance Overlay Sparklines\n71: \n72: - Historical sparkline charts for render timing breakdown\n73: - 60-frame rolling history per metric (clear, highlight, gutter, text, cursor, status, present)\n74: - `draw_sparkline()` function with 1px bar visualization\n75: - `record_render_history()` pushes timing to VecDeque histories\n76: \n77: ### Added - Multi-Cursor Batch Undo/Redo\n78: \n79: - `EditOperation::Batch` for atomic multi-cursor operations\n80: - InsertChar, InsertNewline, DeleteBackward, DeleteForward now batch\n81: - Proper cursor restoration on undo/redo\n82: - 6 new tests for multi-cursor undo behavior\n83: \n84: ### Added - SelectAllOccurrences (Cmd+Shift+L)\n85: \n86: - Finds all occurrences of word/selection in document\n87: - Creates cursor+selection for each occurrence\n88: - Status message shows count: \"Selected N occurrences\"\n89: \n90: ### Added - Layout Tests\n91: \n92: - 47 new tests in `tests/layout.rs`\n93: - Split operations, close operations, focus navigation\n94: - Tab operations (close, move, switch)\n95: - Independent viewport/cursor per editor\n96: - Edge cases (nested splits, invalid IDs)\n97: \n98: ### Changed\n99: \n100: - Test count: 351 (was 246)\n101: - Added 47 layout tests, 6 multi-cursor undo tests, selection tests\n102: \n103: ---\n104: \n105: ## 2025-12-06\n106: \n107: ### Added - Caret Count in Status Bar\n108: \n109: - Shows \"X carets\" segment when multiple cursors are active\n110: - New `SegmentId::CaretCount` variant\n111: - Auto-syncs via `sync_status_bar()` when cursor count changes\n112: \n113: ### Fixed - Multi-Cursor Click Modifier\n114: \n115: - Changed from Cmd+Click to Option+Click for adding/removing cursors\n116: - Matches standard macOS editor conventions\n117: \n118: ### Added - Click+Drag Selection\n119: \n120: - Standard click-and-drag text selection with left mouse button\n121: - `left_mouse_down` state tracking in App struct\n122: - CursorMoved handler extends selection while dragging\n123: - Reuses existing `ExtendSelectionToPosition` message\n124: \n125: ### Added - Delete Line Command\n126: \n127: - `DocumentMsg::DeleteLine` for deleting entire current line\n128: - Cmd+Backspace keybinding (Ctrl+Backspace on non-Mac)\n129: - Smart cursor positioning after delete:\n130:   - First/middle line: stays on same line number\n131:   - Last line: moves to end of previous line\n132:   - Empty line after trailing newline: moves up\n133: - Full undo/redo support\n134: - 8 new tests in `tests/text_editing.rs`\n135: \n136: ### Added - Duplicate Line/Selection (Cmd+D)\n137: \n138: - `DocumentMsg::Duplicate` for duplicating current line or selection\n139: - No selection: duplicates entire line below cursor\n140: - With selection: duplicates selected text in place\n141: - Full undo/redo support\n142: - 4 new tests in `tests/text_editing.rs`\n143: \n144: ### Added - Atomic Replace for Selection Editing\n145: \n146: - `EditOperation::Replace` variant for atomic undo of selection replacement\n147: - When typing over selection, undo restores both deleted text and removes inserted text in one operation\n148: - Prevents \"two-step undo\" bug where user had to undo twice\n149: \n150: ### Fixed - Undo/Redo Keybindings on macOS\n151: \n152: - Cmd+Z now properly triggers Undo (was inserting 'z')\n153: - Cmd+Shift+Z now properly triggers Redo\n154: - Fixed by adding `logo` modifier support alongside `ctrl`\n155: \n156: ### Fixed - Overflow Panics in Edge Cases\n157: \n158: - `move_cursor_down()`: Fixed overflow when `visible_lines` is 0\n159: - `ensure_cursor_visible_with_mode()`: Fixed horizontal scroll overflow\n160: - `StatusBarLayout`: Fixed separator position overflow\n161: - All arithmetic now uses `saturating_add`/`saturating_sub`\n162: \n163: ### Added - Expanded Monkey Tests\n164: \n165: - 12 new window resize edge case tests in `tests/monkey_tests.rs`:\n166:   - Maximum u32 dimensions\n167:   - Very wide/narrow and very tall/narrow\n168:   - Resize then cursor movement/scrolling\n169:   - Oscillating zero/non-zero sizes\n170:   - Resize with active selection\n171:   - Cursor beyond viewport after resize\n172:   - Powers of two dimensions\n173:   - Interleaved resize and text operations\n174:   - Status bar edge (height = line_height)\n175: \n176: ### Added - Status Bar Click Capture\n177: \n178: - Clicks on status bar no longer propagate to editor\n179: - `Renderer::is_in_status_bar(y)` method for hit testing\n180: \n181: ### Changed\n182: \n183: - Test count: 246 (was 227)\n184: - Added 8 delete line tests, 4 duplicate tests, 12 resize tests\n185: \n186: ---\n187: \n188: ## 2025-12-06\n189: \n190: ### Added - Status Bar System\n191: \n192: - Structured, segment-based status bar per `docs/feature/STATUS_BAR.md`\n193: - `StatusBar`, `StatusSegment`, `SegmentId`, `SegmentContent` types\n194: - `sync_status_bar()` auto-updates segments from model state\n195: - `StatusBarLayout` for rendering with separator positions\n196: - Transient messages with auto-expiry (`TransientMessage`)\n197: - Left/right segment alignment with separators\n198: - 47 new status bar tests (`tests/status_bar.rs`)\n199: \n200: ### Added - Overlay System\n201: \n202: - Reusable overlay rendering module (`src/overlay.rs`)\n203: - `OverlayAnchor` enum (TopLeft, TopRight, BottomLeft, BottomRight, Center)\n204: - `OverlayConfig` with builder pattern for configuration\n205: - `render_overlay_background()` with alpha blending\n206: - `render_overlay_border()` for optional 1px borders\n207: - `blend_pixel()` for ARGB alpha compositing\n208: - 7 overlay unit tests\n209: \n210: ### Added - Overlay Theme Integration\n211: \n212: - `OverlayTheme` with themed colors: background, foreground, highlight, warning, error, border\n213: - `OverlayThemeData` for YAML parsing (all fields optional for backward compatibility)\n214: - Perf overlay now uses theme colors instead of hardcoded values\n215: - Optional border rendering when theme specifies border color\n216: - Added overlay sections to all 4 theme files\n217: \n218: ### Fixed\n219: \n220: - Status bar separator lines now span full height (was inset 4px)\n221: - Direction-aware scroll reveal with `ScrollRevealMode` enum\n222: - `ensure_cursor_visible_with_mode()` primitive for scroll behavior\n223: - Arrow key viewport snap-back behavior\n224: - MoveCursor now properly calls ensure_cursor_visible()\n225: - Directional reveal: Up→TopAligned, Down→BottomAligned for natural UX\n226: \n227: ### Changed\n228: \n229: - Test count: 227 (was 185)\n230: - Added 11 scroll reveal tests, 47 status bar tests, 7 overlay tests\n231: \n232: ---\n233: \n234: ## 2025-12-05\n235: \n236: ### Added - Selection & Multi-Cursor (Phase 7)\n237: \n238: #### Phase 7.1: Basic Selection\n239: \n240: - Theme support for `selection_background` and `secondary_cursor_color`\n241: - ~25 new EditorMsg variants for selection/multi-cursor operations\n242: - Shift+Arrow extends selection, Shift+Home/End, Shift+Click\n243: - Selection rendering with blue highlight\n244: - Escape clears selection or collapses multi-cursor\n245: \n246: #### Phase 7.2: Selection Editing\n247: \n248: - `delete_selection()` helper for selection range deletion\n249: - InsertChar/InsertNewline deletes selection before inserting\n250: - DeleteBackward/DeleteForward deletes selection instead of single char\n251: \n252: #### Phase 7.3: Word & Line Selection\n253: \n254: - SelectWord handler using `char_type` for word boundaries\n255: - SelectLine handler (selects entire line including newline)\n256: - Double-click selects word, triple-click selects line\n257: - Click count tracking with wrap at 4\n258: \n259: #### Phase 7.4: Multi-Cursor Basics\n260: \n261: - `toggle_cursor_at()` in EditorState\n262: - ToggleCursorAtPosition handler for Cmd+Click\n263: - Multi-cursor rendering (primary=white, secondary=semi-transparent)\n264: \n265: #### Phase 7.5: Multi-Cursor Editing\n266: \n267: - `cursors_in_reverse_order()` helper\n268: - InsertChar/InsertNewline at all cursors in reverse order\n269: - DeleteBackward/DeleteForward at all cursors in reverse order\n270: \n271: #### Phase 7.6: Clipboard\n272: \n273: - arboard dependency for clipboard support\n274: - Copy (Cmd+C) - copies selection or entire line\n275: - Cut (Cmd+X) - copies and deletes selection\n276: - Paste (Cmd+V) - multi-cursor aware, line-per-cursor distribution\n277: \n278: #### Phase 7.7: Rectangle Selection\n279: \n280: - `RectangleSelectionState` in EditorState\n281: - Middle mouse down starts rectangle mode\n282: - Mouse drag updates rectangle, mouse up finishes\n283: - Creates cursors/selections for each line in rectangle\n284: - Semantics\n50: \n51: A selection has two key positions:\n52: \n53: - **Anchor**: Where the selection started (fixed)\n54: - **Head**: Where the cursor currently is (moves with arrow keys)\n55: \n56: ```\n57: Forward selection (head after anchor):\n58:     anchor          head\n59:        │              │\n60:        ▼              ▼\n61:        Hello, World!\n62:        └──────────────┘\n63:          selected text\n64: \n65: Backward selection (head before anchor):\n66:        head          anchor\n67:        │              │\n68:        ▼              ▼\n69:        Hello, World!\n70:        └──────────────┘\n71:          selected text\n72: ```\n73: \n74: **Direction preservation** is critical:\n75: \n76: - Shift+Right with forward selection: head moves right\n77: - Shift+Left with forward selection: head moves left (may cross anchor)\n78: - When head crosses anchor, selection reverses direction\n79: \n80: ### Selection State\n81: \n82: Each cursor has a parallel selection:\n83: \n84: ```rust\n85: // In EditorState\n86: pub cursors: Vec<Cursor>,      // Cursor positions\n87: pub selections: Vec<Selection>, // Parallel selections (cursors[i] pairs with selections[i])\n88: \n89: // Invariant: cursors.len() == selections.len()\n90: // Invariant: cursors[i].to_position() == selections[i].head\n91: ```\n92: \n93: ### Selection Operations\n94: \n95: ```rust\n96: impl Selection {\n97:     /// Extend selection by moving head to new position\n98:     pub fn extend_to(&mut self, new_head: Position) {\n99:         self.head = new_head;\n100:     }\n101: \n102:     /// Collapse selection to cursor position (clear selection)\n103:     pub fn collapse_to_head(&mut self) {\n104:         self.anchor = self.head;\n105:     }\n106: \n107:     /// Collapse to anchor (used when pressing left with selection)\n108:     pub fn collapse_to_start(&mut self) {\n109:         let start = self.start();\n110:         self.anchor = start;\n111:         self.head = start;\n112:     }\n113: \n114:     /// Collapse to end (used when pressing right with selection)\n115:     pub fn collapse_to_end(&mut self) {\n116:         let end = self.end();\n117:         self.anchor = end;\n118:         self.head = end;\n119:     }\n120: \n121:     /// Get selected text from document\n122:     pub fn get_text(&self, document: &Document) -> String {\n123:         if self.is_empty() {\n124:             return String::new();\n125:         }\n126:         let start = self.start();\n127:         let end = self.end();\n128:         let start_offset = document.cursor_to_offset(start.line, start.column);\n129:         let end_offset = document.cursor_to_offset(end.line, end.column);\n130:         document.buffer.slice(start_offset..end_offset).to_string()\n131:     }\n132: \n133:     /// Check if position is within selection\n134:     pub fn contains(&self, pos: Position) -> bool {\n135: \n161: When moving **without** Shift and a selection exists:\n162: \n163: - **Left/Up**: Collapse to selection start, then move\n164: - **Right/Down**: Collapse to selection end, then move\n165: Move head left, anchor stays\n172:         let new_pos = compute_position_left(selection.head, &model.document);\n173:         selection.head = new_pos;\n174:         model.editor.cursor_mut().line = order** (highest offset first) to prevent edits from shifting subsequent cursor positions.\n274: \n275: ```rust\n276: /// Get cursor indices sorted by document offset descending\n277: fn cursors_in_edit_order(editor: &EditorState, document: &Document) -> Vec<usize> {\n278:     let mut indices: Vec<usize> = (0..editor.cursors.len()).collect();\n279:     indices.sort_by(|&a, &b| {\n280:         let offset_a = document.cursor_to_offset(\n281:             editor.cursors[a].line,\n282:             editor.cursors[a].column\n283:         );\n284:         let offset_b = document.cursor_to_offset(\n285:             editor.cursors[b].line,\n286:             editor.cursors[b].column\n287:         );\n288:         offset_b.cmp(&offset_a)  // Descending\n289:     });\n290:     indices\n291: }\n292: ```\n293: \n294: ### Insert Character at All Cursors\n295: \n296: ```rust\n297: fn insert_char_multi(model: &mut AppModel, ch: char) {\n298:     let indices = cursors_in_edit_order(&model.editor, &model.document);\n299: \n300:     for &i in &indices {\n301:         let cursor = &model.editor.cursors[i];\n302:         let selection = &model.editor.selections[i];\n303: \n304:         // If selection exists, delete it first\n305:         if !selection.is_empty() {\n306:             delete_selection(model, i);\n307:         }\n308: \n309:         // Insert character\n310:         let pos = model.document.cursor_to_offset(cursor.line, cursor.column);\n311:         model.document.buffer.insert_char(pos, ch);\n312: \n313:         // Move cursor right\n314:         model.editor.cursors[i].column += 1;\n315:         model.editor.selections[i] = Selection::new(\n316:             model.editor.cursors[i].to_position()\n317:         );\n318:     }\n319: \n320:     // Record undo operation (combined for all cursors)\n321:     // ...\n322: \n323:     deduplicate_cursors(model);\n324: }\n325: ```\n326: \n327: ### Cursor Deduplication\n328: \n329: After operations, cursors may overlap. Remove duplicates while preserving primary cursor (index 0).\n330: \n331: ```rust\n332: fn deduplicate_cursors(model: &mut AppModel) {\n333:     let mut seen: HashSet<(usize, usize)> = HashSet::new();\n334:     let mut to_remove: Vec<usize> = Vec::new();\n335: \n336:     // Always keep primary cursor\n337:     seen.insert((model.editor.cursors[0].line, model.editor.cursors[0].column));\n338: \n339:     for i in 1..model.editor.cursors.len() {\n340:         let key = (model.editor.cursors[i].line, model.editor.cursors[i].column);\n341:         if seen.contains(&key) {\n342:             to_remove.push(i);\n343:         } else {\n344:             seen.insert(key);\n345:         }\n346:     }\n347: \n348:     // Remove in reverse order to preserve indices\n349:     for i in to_remove.into_iter().rev() {\n350:         model.editor.cursors.remove(i);\n351:         model.editor.selections.remove(i);\n352:     }\n353: }\n354: ```\n355: \n356: ### Selection Merging\n357: \n358: When selections overlap, merge them:\n359: \n360: ```rust\n361: fn merge_overlapping_selections(editor: &mut EditorState) {\n362:     if editor.selections.len() <= 1 {\n363:         return;\n364:     }\n365: \n366:     // Sort by start position\n367:     let mut indexed: Vec<(usize, Selection)> = editor.selections\n368:         .iter()\n369:         .cloned()\n370:         .enumerate()\n371:         .collect();\n372:     indexed.sort_by_key(|(_, s)| s.start());\n373: \n374:     let mut merged: Vec<(usize, Selection)> = Vec::new();\n375: \n376:     for (i, sel) in indexed {\n377:         if let Some((_, last)) = merged.last_mut() {\n378:             if sel.start() <= last.end() {\n379:                 // Overlapping - extend last selection\n380:                 if sel.end() > last.end() {\n381:                     last.head = sel.end();  // Extend to cover both\n382:                 }\n383:                 // Remove the cursor for this merged selection\n384:                 // (handled separately)\n385:                 continue;\n386:             }\n387:         }\n388:         merged.push((i, sel));\n389:     }\n390: \n391:     // Rebuild cursors and selections from merged set\n392:     // Keep only cursors whose selections weren't merged away\n393:     // ...\n394: }\n395: ```\n396: \n397: ---\n398: Perfect! Now I have all the information needed to provide a comprehensive answer. Let me compile the findings:

## Summary

Based on my analysis of the codebase, here are the key findings regarding multi-cursor movement in the Rust editor:

### **Critical Finding: Cursor Movement Operations Only Move the PRIMARY Cursor**

All cursor movement operations (`MoveCursor`, `MoveCursorUp/Down/Left/Right`, `MoveCursorLineStart/End`, `MoveCursorDocumentStart/End`, `MoveCursorWord`) **ONLY move the primary cursor (index 0)**, NOT all cursors.

### Key Files:

1. **`~/code/rust-editor/src/update.rs`** (lines 73-159, 696-820)
   - Contains all cursor movement handlers
   - Uses `.cursor_mut()` which always refers to `&mut cursors[0]`
   - Movement functions: `move_cursor_up()`, `move_cursor_down()`, `move_cursor_left()`, `move_cursor_right()`, `move_cursor_word_left()`, `move_cursor_word_right()`
   - All call `.collapse_selections_to_cursors()` after movement (applies to ALL cursors)

2. **`~/code/rust-editor/src/messages.rs`** (lines 18-110)
   - Defines `EditorMsg` enum with movement messages
   - Note: Movement operations don't include any multi-cursor specification

3. **`~/code/rust-editor/src/model/editor.rs`** (lines 324-335, 372-378)
   - `cursor()` method returns `&cursors[0]` (primary cursor only)
   - `cursor_mut()` method returns `&mut cursors[0]` (primary cursor only)
   - `collapse_selections_to_cursors()` updates ALL cursors' selections

### Movement Operations That Only Affect Primary Cursor:

- `MoveCursor(Direction)` - Up/Down/Left/Right
- `MoveCursorLineStart` - Home key
- `MoveCursorLineEnd` - End key
- `MoveCursorDocumentStart` - Ctrl+Home
- `MoveCursorDocumentEnd` - Ctrl+End
- `MoveCursorWord(Direction)` - Word navigation
- `PageUp` / `PageDown`
- `MoveCursorWithSelection(Direction)` - Shift+Arrow keys
- `MoveCursorLineStartWithSelection` - Shift+Home
- `MoveCursorLineEndWithSelection` - Shift+End
- All document start/end variants with selection

### Multi-Cursor Operations (These DO affect multiple cursors):

Located in `~/code/rust-editor/src/update.rs` (lines 580-650):

1. **`AddCursorAbove`** (lines 587-607)
   - Iterates through ALL existing cursors
   - Adds a cursor on the line above each one
   - Respects `desired_column`

2. **`AddCursorBelow`** (lines 609-630)
   - Iterates through ALL existing cursors
   - Adds a cursor on the line below each one
   - Maintains `desired_column`

3. **`CollapseToSingleCursor`** (lines 632-637)
   - Removes all but the primary cursor
   - Triggered by Escape key with multiple cursors

4. **`RemoveCursor(index)`** (lines 639-649)
   - Removes a specific cursor by index
   - Prevents removing the only cursor

5. **`ToggleCursorAtPosition`** (lines 581-585)
   - Adds/removes cursor at a specific position (Option+Click)

6. **Text editing operations with multiple cursors** (lines 1250-1440 in update.rs)
   - Batch operations that process ALL cursors in reverse document order
   - Used for `InsertChar`, `DeleteBackward`, `DeleteForward` with multi-cursor

### Important Method: `collapse_selections_to_cursors()`

**This is called after EVERY cursor movement operation** (update.rs lines 82, 102, 117, 128, 143, 155, 177, 206, 217):

```rust
pub fn collapse_selections_to_cursors(&mut self) {
    for (cursor, selection) in self.cursors.iter().zip(self.selections.iter_mut()) {
        let pos = cursor.to_position();
        selection.anchor = pos;
        selection.head = pos;
    }
}
```

This updates **all cursors' selections** but only after the **primary cursor** has been moved.

### Architecture Notes:

- EditorState supports infrastructure for multiple cursors (`cursors: Vec<Cursor>`, `selections: Vec<Selection>`)
- Multi-cursor operations are limited to: adding cursors, removing cursors, collapsing to single cursor, and text editing
- **Cursor movement is intentionally single-cursor** (primary cursor only)
- When there are multiple cursors, movement only affects the primary cursor; other cursors remain in place
```

Implement multi-cursor movement by refactoring the existing single-cursor movement helpers to operate on all `(cursor, selection)` pairs, preserving per-cursor `desired_column`, and running `deduplicate_cursors()` after each logical movement. Then wire all movement-related `EditorMsg` handlers to these helpers, with separate variants for “with selection”.

Effort: **M (1–3h)** once you know the codebase.

---

2) Recommended approach (simple path)

### P0 – Establish core helpers for multi-cursor movement

**Goal:** Centralize all movement logic so all handlers call the same multi-cursor-aware helpers.

a. **Introduce per-cursor primitives (index-based)**

In `editor.rs` (or `update.rs` if you prefer to keep movement logic there), add internal helpers that operate on a *single cursor by index*:

```rust
impl EditorState {
    fn move_cursor_left_at(&mut self, doc: &Document, idx: usize) { /* copy current logic, but use self.cursors[idx] */ }
    fn move_cursor_right_at(&mut self, doc: &Document, idx: usize) { /* ... */ }
    fn move_cursor_up_at(&mut self, doc: &Document, idx: usize) { /* uses desired_column */ }
    fn move_cursor_down_at(&mut self, doc: &Document, idx: usize) { /* uses desired_column */ }

    fn move_cursor_word_left_at(&mut self, doc: &Document, idx: usize) { /* existing word-move logic generalized */ }
    fn move_cursor_word_right_at(&mut self, doc: &Document, idx: usize) { /* ... */ }

    fn move_cursor_line_start_at(&mut self, doc: &Document, idx: usize) { /* per-line first-non-ws & 0 toggling */ }
    fn move_cursor_line_end_at(&mut self, doc: &Document, idx: usize) { /* per-line last-non-ws & line_len toggling */ }

    fn move_cursor_document_start_at(&mut self, idx: usize) { /* line=0,col=0, desired_column=None */ }
    fn move_cursor_document_end_at(&mut self, doc: &Document, idx: usize) { /* last line, end of line */ }

    fn page_up_at(&mut self, doc: &Document, jump: usize, idx: usize) { /* line -= jump, clamp; desired_column per cursor */ }
    fn page_down_at(&mut self, doc: &Document, jump: usize, idx: usize) { /* line += jump, clamp; desired_column per cursor */ }
}
```

Concretely:

- Take the existing bodies of `move_cursor_left`, `move_cursor_right`, `move_cursor_up`, `move_cursor_down`, `move_cursor_word_left`, `move_cursor_word_right`, `MoveCursorLineStart`, `MoveCursorLineEnd`, `MoveCursorDocumentStart`, `MoveCursorDocumentEnd`, `PageUp`, `PageDown`.
- Replace any `editor.cursor_mut()` / `editor.cursor()` with `self.cursors[idx]` / `&self.cursors[idx]`.
- Replace `model.current_line_length()`, `model.first_non_whitespace_column()`, `model.last_non_whitespace_column()` with per-line computations using `doc` and `self.cursors[idx].line` (see P1 below).
- Use `self.cursors[idx].remember_column()` / `clear_desired_column()` where appropriate for vertical moves.

b. **Introduce “all-cursors” helpers**

Still in `editor.rs` (recommended) or `update.rs`:

```rust
impl EditorState {
    fn move_all_cursors_left(&mut self, doc: &Document) {
        for i in 0..self.cursors.len() {
            self.move_cursor_left_at(doc, i);
        }
        self.deduplicate_cursors();
    }

    fn move_all_cursors_right(&mut self, doc: &Document) { /* same pattern */ }
    fn move_all_cursors_up(&mut self, doc: &Document) { /* uses ..._up_at */ }
    fn move_all_cursors_down(&mut self, doc: &Document) { /* uses ..._down_at */ }

    fn move_all_cursors_word_left(&mut self, doc: &Document) { /* ... */ }
    fn move_all_cursors_word_right(&mut self, doc: &Document) { /* ... */ }

    fn move_all_cursors_line_start(&mut self, doc: &Document) { /* ... */ }
    fn move_all_cursors_line_end(&mut self, doc: &Document) { /* ... */ }

    fn move_all_cursors_document_start(&mut self, doc: &Document) {
        for i in 0..self.cursors.len() {
            self.move_cursor_document_start_at(i);
        }
        self.deduplicate_cursors();
    }

    fn move_all_cursors_document_end(&mut self, doc: &Document) { /* ... */ }

    fn page_up_all_cursors(&mut self, doc: &Document, jump: usize) { /* per cursor; dedupe */ }
    fn page_down_all_cursors(&mut self, doc: &Document, jump: usize) { /* per cursor; dedupe */ }
}
```

Key rule: **every multi-cursor movement helper should end with `self.deduplicate_cursors()`** so collisions are resolved consistently.

---

### P1 – Fix basic movement handlers (`MoveCursor` + Home/End/Doc)

**Goal:** All arrow/Home/End/Doc movements move every cursor while preserving selections correctly for the non-Shift case.

1. **Update `EditorMsg::MoveCursor` handler**

In `update_editor`:

```rust
EditorMsg::MoveCursor(direction) => {
    {
        let doc = model.document();
        let editor = model.editor_mut();
        match direction {
            Direction::Up => editor.move_all_cursors_up(doc),
            Direction::Down => editor.move_all_cursors_down(doc),
            Direction::Left => editor.move_all_cursors_left(doc),
            Direction::Right => editor.move_all_cursors_right(doc),
        }
        // selections collapsed for ALL cursors
        editor.collapse_selections_to_cursors();
    }
    // viewport logic can still use primary cursor only
    let vertical_hint = match direction {
        Direction::Up => Some(true),
        Direction::Down => Some(false),
        _ => None,
    };
    model.ensure_cursor_visible_directional(vertical_hint);
    model.reset_cursor_blink();
    Some(Cmd::Redraw)
}
```

2. **Update `MoveCursorLineStart` / `MoveCursorLineEnd`**

Instead of reading/modifying only `cursor_mut()`:

```rust
EditorMsg::MoveCursorLineStart => {
    {
        let doc = model.document();
        let editor = model.editor_mut();
        editor.move_all_cursors_line_start(doc);
        // clear desired_column for all
        for c in &mut editor.cursors {
            c.desired_column = None;
        }
        editor.collapse_selections_to_cursors();
    }
    model.ensure_cursor_visible();
    model.reset_cursor_blink();
    Some(Cmd::Redraw)
}
```

Same pattern for `MoveCursorLineEnd`.

3. **Update `MoveCursorDocumentStart` / `MoveCursorDocumentEnd`**

Use new all-cursors helpers:

```rust
EditorMsg::MoveCursorDocumentStart => {
    {
        let doc = model.document();
        let editor = model.editor_mut();
        editor.move_all_cursors_document_start(doc);
        // desired_column cleared in helper
        editor.collapse_selections_to_cursors();
    }
    // viewport/top_line logic can still use primary cursor as today
    model.ensure_cursor_visible();
    model.reset_cursor_blink();
    Some(Cmd::Redraw)
}
```

Same for `MoveCursorDocumentEnd` (keeping the existing viewport/top_line logic but based on primary cursor after movement).

4. **Per-line whitespace helpers (needed for line start/end)**

Currently `model.first_non_whitespace_column()` / `model.last_non_whitespace_column()` presumably use **primary** cursor’s line. For multi-cursor, add per-line helpers in `Document` or `EditorState`:

```rust
fn first_non_whitespace_column_for_line(doc: &Document, line: usize) -> usize { /* scan chars */ }
fn last_non_whitespace_column_for_line(doc: &Document, line: usize) -> usize { /* scan from end */ }
```

Then in `move_cursor_line_start_at` / `move_cursor_line_end_at` use:

```rust
let line = self.cursors[idx].line;
let first_non_ws = first_non_whitespace_column_for_line(doc, line);
if self.cursors[idx].column == first_non_ws {
    self.cursors[idx].column = 0;
} else {
    self.cursors[idx].column = first_non_ws;
}
self.cursors[idx].desired_column = None;
```

This ensures multi-cursor Home/End behave correctly per line.

---

### P2 – Multi-cursor word movement and paging

**Goal:** Word-wise and page-wise movement respects all cursors and per-cursor `desired_column`.

1. **Update `EditorMsg::MoveCursorWord`**

```rust
EditorMsg::MoveCursorWord(direction) => {
    {
        let doc = model.document();
        let editor = model.editor_mut();
        match direction {
            Direction::Left => editor.move_all_cursors_word_left(doc),
            Direction::Right => editor.move_all_cursors_word_right(doc),
            _ => {}
        }
        editor.collapse_selections_to_cursors();
    }
    model.ensure_cursor_visible();
    model.reset_cursor_blink();
    Some(Cmd::Redraw)
}
```

Implement `move_cursor_word_left_at` / `_right_at` by generalizing existing single-cursor word-move logic (use `char_type`, scan on the cursor’s line, clamp by line length).

2. **Update `PageUp` / `PageDown`**

Instead of moving only primary:

```rust
EditorMsg::PageUp => {
    let jump = model.editor().viewport.visible_lines.saturating_sub(2);
    {
        let doc = model.document();
        let editor = model.editor_mut();

        // For each cursor:
        editor.page_up_all_cursors(doc, jump);

        // Viewport: still adjust using primary cursor only (as current logic)
        editor.viewport.top_line =
            editor.viewport.top_line.saturating_sub(jump);
        editor.collapse_selections_to_cursors();
    }
    model.ensure_cursor_visible_directional(Some(true));
    model.reset_cursor_blink();
    Some(Cmd::Redraw)
}
```

`page_up_all_cursors` should:

- For each cursor:
  - `cursor.remember_column()` (if `desired_column` is `None`).
  - Compute new `line = cursor.line.saturating_sub(jump)`.
  - Compute `desired = cursor.desired_column.unwrap_or(cursor.column)`.
  - Set `cursor.column = desired.min(doc.line_length(line))`.
- Keep `desired_column = Some(desired)` (as currently done).
- Finally `deduplicate_cursors()`.

Do the symmetric thing for `PageDown`.

---

### P3 – Multi-cursor movement *with* selection (Shift variants)

**Goal:** For all Shift+movement variants, move each cursor and update each corresponding selection head, preserving anchors and `desired_column`.

1. **Core pattern for “with selection”**

Add helpers in `EditorState` that *wrap* the movement primitives and update selection heads:

```rust
impl EditorState {
    fn move_all_cursors_with_selection<F>(&mut self, doc: &Document, mut move_fn: F)
    where
        F: FnMut(&mut EditorState, &Document, usize),
    {
        for i in 0..self.cursors.len() {
            // Movement
            move_fn(self, doc, i);
            // Selection head follows cursor
            let pos = self.cursors[i].to_position();
            self.selections[i].head = pos;
        }
        self.deduplicate_cursors();
    }
}
```

Now implement concrete helpers:

```rust
fn move_all_cursors_left_with_selection(&mut self, doc: &Document) {
    self.move_all_cursors_with_selection(doc, EditorState::move_cursor_left_at);
}
fn move_all_cursors_right_with_selection(&mut self, doc: &Document) { /* ... */ }
fn move_all_cursors_up_with_selection(&mut self, doc: &Document) { /* ... uses _up_at */ }
fn move_all_cursors_down_with_selection(&mut self, doc: &Document) { /* ... */ }
// word, line start/end, doc start/end, page up/down analogously
```

2. **Update `EditorMsg::MoveCursorWithSelection`**

```rust
EditorMsg::MoveCursorWithSelection(direction) => {
    {
        let doc = model.document();
        let editor = model.editor_mut();
        match direction {
            Direction::Up => editor.move_all_cursors_up_with_selection(doc),
            Direction::Down => editor.move_all_cursors_down_with_selection(doc),
            Direction::Left => editor.move_all_cursors_left_with_selection(doc),
            Direction::Right => editor.move_all_cursors_right_with_selection(doc),
        }
        // DO NOT collapse selections here (these are selection-expanding moves)
    }
    let vertical_hint = match direction {
        Direction::Up => Some(true),
        Direction::Down => Some(false),
        _ => None,
    };
    model.ensure_cursor_visible_directional(vertical_hint);
    model.reset_cursor_blink();
    Some(Cmd::Redraw)
}
```

3. **Update line/document/word/page “with selection” variants**

For each of:

- `MoveCursorLineStartWithSelection`
- `MoveCursorLineEndWithSelection`
- `MoveCursorDocumentStartWithSelection`
- `MoveCursorDocumentEndWithSelection`
- `MoveCursorWordWithSelection(Direction)`
- `PageUpWithSelection`
- `PageDownWithSelection`

Use the matching `*_with_selection` helpers instead of touching only `cursor_mut()` and single `selection_mut()`.

Example – line start with selection:

```rust
EditorMsg::MoveCursorLineStartWithSelection => {
    {
        let doc = model.document();
        let editor = model.editor_mut();
        editor.move_all_cursors_line_start_with_selection(doc);
        // desired_column already handled inside movement primitive
    }
    model.ensure_cursor_visible();
    model.reset_cursor_blink();
    Some(Cmd::Redraw)
}
```

Semantics: anchors per-selection remain untouched, only `head` and corresponding `cursor` move.

Note: because `collapse_selections_to_cursors()` is **not** called on these code paths (by design), multi-cursor selections behave as expected.

---

### P4 – Cursor deduplication strategy

**Goal:** Ensure collisions between cursors are consistently resolved.

You already have:

```rust
pub fn deduplicate_cursors(&mut self) { /* keeps first occurrence, fixes selections */ }
```

Apply this rule:

- All movement helpers that can cause collisions (**all multi-cursor moves**) must call `deduplicate_cursors()` **exactly once at the end**.
- Do **not** call it inside per-cursor `*_at` functions; only in the “all-cursors” wrappers.

This ensures:

- For non-Shift moves, `collapse_selections_to_cursors()` will run after dedup, preserving the selection invariant.
- For Shift-moves, dedup removes duplicate cursor/selection pairs but preserves the first one’s anchor/head; the others disappear entirely (cursors + selections), which is intuitive.

---

### P5 – Viewport behavior

**Goal:** Keep behavior simple: still base viewport on primary cursor.

You don’t need to change viewport code initially:

- `ensure_cursor_visible()` and `ensure_cursor_visible_directional(...)` already rely only on `cursors[0]`.
- After moving all cursors, primary will be one of them; the others may be off-screen, which is a reasonable first implementation.

If desired later, you could add options to center the viewport around the “median” cursor or ensure **any** cursor is visible, but that’s beyond the current requirement.

---

3) Rationale and trade-offs

- **Centralizing movement logic** avoids a combinatorial explosion of bugs: instead of trying to retrofit multi-cursor logic into each handler, you create a single set of primitives (`*_at`, `move_all_*`, `move_all_*_with_selection`) and call them everywhere.
- **Index-based helpers** reuse existing, well-tested single-cursor algorithms with minimal changes, which lowers the risk of subtle off-by-one or word-boundary bugs.
- **Per-cursor `desired_column`** matches user expectations when moving multi-cursors vertically: each cursor “remembers” its own visual column through ragged lines.
- **Dedup-at-end** is simple and works well with the current `deduplicate_cursors()` implementation; there’s no need for more complex merging strategies yet.

Alternatives (not chosen now):

- Push all movement logic into methods on `Cursor` that are completely document-agnostic. This would require passing more document helpers around and duplicating some logic; not necessary for the current scope.
- Making viewport follow “extreme” cursor (topmost or bottommost) for Up/Down; more opinionated and not clearly better than using primary.

---

4) Risks and guardrails

- **Risk: breaking single-cursor behavior.**
  Guardrail: test all movements (arrows, Home/End, Ctrl+Home/End, word, page up/down, with and without Shift) with only one cursor to ensure behavior hasn’t changed.

- **Risk: inconsistent selection invariants.**
  Guardrail: after each movement handler, in debug builds, call `editor.assert_invariants()` or rely on its existing use. This will catch `cursor`/`selection.head` mismatches early.

- **Risk: incorrect per-line Home/End for multi-line cursors.**
  Guardrail: make sure you replaced `first_non_whitespace_column()` / `last_non_whitespace_column()` with per-line versions that read `cursor.line` rather than primary.

- **Risk: performance with many cursors.**
  For now, a trivial `for` loop is fine; if users start using thousands of cursors, profile and optimize later.

---

5) When to consider the advanced path

Revisit the design if:

- You want **smarter viewport behavior** (e.g., always keeping the highest or lowest cursor visible, or dynamically centering around the “active” cursor).
- You add **rectangular selection–driven cursors** or other exotic selection types that break the 1:1 cursor/selection mapping.
- You introduce **configurable movement semantics** (e.g., Vim-like motions, soft-wrap-aware movement), where centralizing movement logic even further (in a dedicated movement module) becomes important.

---

6) Optional advanced path (outline only)

If you later want a more extensible system:

- Introduce a `CursorMotion` trait with methods like `apply(&self, doc: &Document, cursor: &mut Cursor)` and `apply_with_selection(...)`.
- Represent each movement (`ArrowUp`, `WordLeft`, `LineStart`, etc.) as a value implementing `CursorMotion`.
- Have `EditorState::apply_motion_to_all_cursors(motion: &impl CursorMotion, selection_mode: SelectionMode)` handle the loop, selection updates, and deduplication.
- `update_editor` would map each `EditorMsg` to a `CursorMotion` + `SelectionMode`, greatly simplifying the handler logic.

For now, the simpler index-based helpers + all-cursors wrappers are sufficient and align with the current architecture and requirements.
```

## Assistant

Here's the summary of what was done:

### Summary

#### Multi-Cursor Movement Gap Identified

**Root cause:** All movement handlers use `cursor_mut()` which always returns `&mut cursors[0]` (primary cursor only). Multi-cursor **editing** works (insert/delete), but **movement** doesn't.

#### Created Implementation Plan

[feature/MULTI_CURSOR_MOVEMENT.md](file://~/code/rust-editor/docs/feature/MULTI_CURSOR_MOVEMENT.md) with 5 phases:

| Phase | Description | Effort |
|-------|-------------|--------|
| **0** | Per-cursor primitives (`move_cursor_*_at(idx)`) | Low |
| **1** | All-cursors wrappers (`move_all_cursors_*()`) | Low |
| **2** | Update movement handlers in update.rs | Medium |
| **3** | Selection movement helpers (`*_with_selection`) | Low |
| **4** | Update Shift+Arrow handlers | Low |
| **5** | Per-line whitespace helpers | Low |

**Total effort:** ~1-3 hours

#### Docs Cleaned Up

- **ROADMAP.md:** Reorganized with "In Progress" section for multi-cursor movement, removed verbose completed items
- **CHANGELOG.md:** Added entry documenting the identified gap and plan

#### Key Implementation Points

1. Add index-based helpers to `EditorState`: `move_cursor_left_at(doc, idx)`
2. Add all-cursors wrappers: `move_all_cursors_left(doc)` that iterate + deduplicate
3. Update each `MoveCursor*` handler to use new methods
4. Add selection variants for Shift+movement
5. Every multi-cursor operation ends with `deduplicate_cursors()`

first_non_ws;\n+        }\n+        cursor.desired_column = None;\n+    }\n+\n+    /// Move a single cursor to line end (smart: last non-ws or line end)\n+    pub fn move_cursor_line_end_at(&mut self, doc.last_non_whitespace_column(cursor.line);\n+        if cursor.column == last_non_ws {\n+            cursor.column = line_len;\n+        } else {\n+            cursor.column = last_non_ws;\n+        }\n+        cursor.desired_column = None;\n+    }\n+\n+    /// Move a single cursor to document start\n+    pub fn move_cursor_document_start_at(&mut self, idx: usize) {\n+        let cursor = &mut self.cursors[idx];\n+        cursor.line = 0;\n+        cursor.column = 0;\n+        cursor.desired_column = None;\n+    }\n+\n+    /// Move a single cursor to document end\n+    pub fn move_cursor_document_end_at(&mut self, doc: &Document, idx: usize) {\n+        let cursor = &mut self.cursors[idx];\n+        cursor.line = doc.line_count().saturating_sub(1);\n+        cursor.column = doc.line_length(cursor.line);\n+        cursor.desired_column = None;\n+    }\n+\n+    /// Move a single cursor up by `jump` lines (for page up)\n+    pub fn page_up_at(&mut self, doc: &Document, jump: usize, idx: usize) {\n+        let cursor = &mut self.cursors[idx];\n+        cursor.line = cursor.line.saturating_sub(jump);\n+        let desired = cursor.desired_column.unwrap_or(cursor.column);\n+        let line_len = doc.line_length(cursor.line);\n+        cursor.column = desired.min(line_len);\n+        cursor.desired_column = Some(desired);\n+    }\n+\n+    /// Move a single cursor down by `jump` lines (for page down)\n+    pub fn page_down_at(&mut self, doc: &Document, jump: usize, idx: usize) {\n+        let cursor = &mut self.cursors[idx];\n+        let max_line = doc.line_count().saturating_sub(1);\n+        cursor.line = (cursor.line + jump).min(max_line);\n+        let desired = cursor.desired_column.unwrap_or(cursor.column);\n+        let line_len = doc.line_length(cursor.line);\n+        cursor.column = desired.min(line_len);\n+        cursor.desired_column = Some(desired);\n+    }\n+\n+    /// Move a single cursor one word left\n+    pub fn move_cursor_word_left_at(&mut self, doc: &Document, idx: usize) {\n+        let cursor = &self.cursors[idx];\n+        let pos = doc.cursor_to_offset(cursor.line, cursor.column);\n+        if pos == 0 {\n+            return;\n+        }\n+\n+        let text: String = doc.buffer.slice(..pos).chars().collect();\n+ char_type(chars[i - 1]) == current_type {\n+                i -= 1;\n+            }\n+        }\n+\n+        let (line, column) = doc.offset_to_cursor(i);\n+        let cursor = &mut self.cursors[idx];\n+        cursor.line = line;\n+        cursor.column = column;\n+        cursor.desired_column = None;\n+    }\n+\n+    /// Move a single cursor one word right\n+    pub fn move_cursor_word_right_at(&mut self, doc: &Document, idx: usize) {\n+        let cursor = &self.cursors[idx];\n+        let pos = doc.cursor_to_offset(cursor.line, cursor.column);\n+        let total_chars = doc.buffer.len_chars();\n+        if pos >= total_chars {\n+            return;\n+        }\n+\n+        let text: String = doc.buffer.slice(pos..).chars().collect();\n+        let chars: Vec<char> = text.chars().collect();\n+        let mut i = 0;\n+\n+        if !chars.is_empty() {\n+            let current_type = char_type(chars[0]);\n+            while i < chars.len() && model.editor_mut().selections.push(token::model::Selection::new(token::model::Position::new(0, 6)));\n+\n+    // Shift+Right to extend selection\n+    update(&mut model, Msg::Editor(EditorMsg::MoveCursorWithSelection(Direction::Right)));\n+\n+    assert_eq!(model.editor().cursors.len(), 2);\n+    // Both cursors moved\n+    assert_eq!(model.editor().cursors[0].column,   "diff": "```diff\nIndex: ~/code/rust-editor/docs/CHANGELOG.md\n===================================================================\n--- ~/code/rust-editor/docs/CHANGELOG.md\toriginal\n+++ ~/code/rust-editor/docs/CHANGELOG.md\tmodified\n@@ -5,16 +5,33 @@\n ---\n \n ## 2025-12-06 (Latest)\n \n-### Identified - Multi-Cursor Movement Gap\n+### Added - Multi-Cursor Movement\n \n-Analysis revealed multi-cursor **editing** works but **movement** only affects primary cursor:\n+All cursor movement operations now work with multiple cursors:\n \n-- Root cause: `cursor_mut()` always returns `&mut cursors[0]`\n-- Created implementation plan: [feature/MULTI_CURSOR_MOVEMENT.md](feature/MULTI_CURSOR_MOVEMENT.md)\n-- 5-phase implementation to add per-cursor movement primitives and all-cursors wrappers\n+- **Arrow keys** (Up/Down/Left/Right) move ALL cursors simultaneously\n+- **Home/End** moves all cursors to their respective line starts/ends (smart behavior preserved)\n+- **Word navigation** (Option+Arrow) moves all cursors by word\n+- **Page Up/Down** moves all cursors\n+- **Shift+movement** extends selection for ALL cursors\n+- **Cursor deduplication** when cursors collide after movement\n+- Each cursor preserves its own `desired_column` for vertical movement through ragged lines\n \n+#### Implementation Details\n+\n+- Per-cursor primitives in `EditorState`: `move_cursor_*_at(doc, idx)`\n+- All-cursors wrappers: `move_all_cursors_*(doc)` \n+- Selection variants: `move_all_cursors_*_with_selection(doc)`\n+- Removed legacy single-cursor movement functions from `update.rs`\n+- 10 new multi-cursor movement tests in `tests/cursor_movement.rs`\n+\n+### Changed\n+\n+- Test count: 383 (was 351)\n+- Added 10 multi-cursor movement tests, 22 other improvements\n+\n ---\n \n ## 2025-12-06\n \n```",

## Assistant

Here are the **planned features** in priority order:

| Feature | Complexity | Description |
|---------|------------|-------------|
| **Expand/Shrink Selection** | Small | Option+Up/Down to expand word→line→all, with history stack |
| **File Dropping** | Medium | Drag-and-drop files onto window to open them |
| **Workspace Management** | Large | CLI args (`red file1 file2`), file tree sidebar, FS watching |
| **Codebase Organization** | Medium | Split `main.rs` (~3100 lines) and `update.rs` (~2800 lines) into modules |
| **Undo Coalescing** | Small | Group rapid keystrokes into single undo entries |

**Deferred items** (from Split View):
- Splitter drag resize
- Tab drag-and-drop between groups
- Cursor adjustment when other views edit same document
